    def test_a_marked_sibling_can_evaluate_an_unknown_fixture_tail_before_the_unmarked_unit():
        gate = GATE.replace("not os.environ.get('SLOW')", "touch()")
        units = (UNIT + "\n@pytest.mark.slow\ndef test_second():\n"
                 "    assert total() + 0 == 78.75\n")
        # The first condition is false for test_total, but true for test_second.
        # Its unknown tail can affect later execution even if the fixture then
        # skips that sibling. Do not infer whole-module inactivity per one unit.
        # Only the removed unmarked oracle is the obligation asserted here.
>       assert_blocked(moved(before_tests=units, gate=gate), unit="test_total")
